- **test-massing**: Runs the massing arithmetic for one or more schemes: floor plate area times floor count gives GFA, floor count times floor-to-floor gives height, and an optional net-to-gross factor gives net area, all compared side by side and against targets the user supplies. Trigger on "massing options", "massing study", "how many floors do I need", "GFA check", "compare schemes", "test-massing", or a pasted list of plate areas and floor counts. Pure arithmetic: it carries no zoning limits, no typical floor heights, and no typical efficiency factors. Targets come from the user's own zoning check or brief.… ([DavidCho1999/archaiflow-skills](https://github.com/DavidCho1999/archaiflow-skills/blob/HEAD/bundles/design-building-types/commands/test-massing.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
